Cat · Full-body neuromechanics

Per-channel strength, optimal fiber length, tendon slack length, pennation, damping and force-law settings. Units and source values appear beside the applied entries.

Start here · Find a file

Data/Model_Parameters/muscle_tendon.py

   1 # F0 is maximum isometric MUSCLE force along the tendon direction, in newtons.
   2 # At full activation, optimal fiber length and zero fiber velocity, this is
   3 # the active reference force transmitted along the tendon. The pennation
   4 # projection is already included: F0 = maximum isometric fiber force * cos(alpha).
   5 # This is not tendon breaking strength or a ceiling on total tendon tension.
   6 # Passive fiber force is enabled for all 104 muscle channels.
   7 # Published PE law: Prilutsky et al. (2016), Table 10.3, DOI 10.1007/978-1-4939-3267-2_10;
   8 # Rahmati et al. (2025), Table 3, DOI 10.1113/JP287448.
   9 # Applying the common normalized law to added toe/girdle channels is a model assumption.
  10 # Read this file independently: executable numbers in PARAMETERS are the values used in this model.
  11 # Source-value comments identify verified source numbers; missing sources are explicitly stated.
